<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Public_houses_and_hotels">Public houses and hotels</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Robert_Peel&amp;action=edit&amp;section=25" title="Edit section: Public houses and hotels">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p>The following <a href="/wiki/Public_house" class="mw-redirect" title="Public house">public houses</a>, bars or hotels are named after Peel:<sup id="cite_ref-102" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-102">&#91;102&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="United_Kingdom">United Kingdom</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Robert_Peel&amp;action=edit&amp;section=26" title="Edit section: United Kingdom">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<figure typeof="mw:File/Thumb"><a href="/wiki/File:Sir_Robert_Peel_pub_Leicester.jpg" class="mw-file-description"><img src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/7/7c/Sir_Robert_Peel_pub_Leicester.jpg/250px-Sir_Robert_Peel_pub_Leicester.jpg" decoding="async" width="250" height="188" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/7/7c/Sir_Robert_Peel_pub_Leicester.jpg/375px-Sir_Robert_Peel_pub_Leicester.jpg 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/7/7c/Sir_Robert_Peel_pub_Leicester.jpg/500px-Sir_Robert_Peel_pub_Leicester.jpg 2x" data-file-width="3264" data-file-height="2448" /></a><figcaption>Sir Robert Peel pub, Leicester.</figcaption></figure>
<ul><li>Sir Robert Peel pub Bury, behind his statue Former Wetherspoon.</li>
<li>Sir Robert Peel public house, <a href="/wiki/Tamworth,_Staffordshire" title="Tamworth, Staffordshire">Tamworth</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-103" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-103">&#91;103&#93;</a></sup></li>
<li>Peel Hotel, <a href="/wiki/Tamworth,_Staffordshire" title="Tamworth, Staffordshire">Tamworth</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-104" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-104">&#91;104&#93;</a></sup></li>
<li>Sir Robert Peel public house, Edgeley, Stockport, Cheshire.</li>
<li>Sir Robert Peel public house, <a href="/wiki/Heckmondwike" title="Heckmondwike">Heckmondwike</a>, West Yorkshire.</li>
<li>Sir Robert Peel public house,<sup id="cite_ref-105" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-105">&#91;105&#93;</a></sup> <a href="/wiki/Leicester" title="Leicester">Leicester</a>.</li>
<li>Sir Robert Peel public house, Malden Road, London NW5.</li>
<li>Sir Robert Peel public house, Peel Precinct, Kilburn, London NW6.</li>
<li>Sir Robert Peel public house, London SE17.</li>
<li>Sir Robert Peel Hotel, <a href="/wiki/Preston,_Lancashire" title="Preston, Lancashire">Preston</a>.</li>
<li>Peel Park Hotel, Accrington, Lancashire.</li>
<li>Sir Robert Peel public house <a href="/wiki/Rowley_Regis" title="Rowley Regis">Rowley Regis</a>.</li>
<li>Sir Robert Peel public house, <a href="/wiki/Southsea" title="Southsea">Southsea</a>.</li>
<li>Sir Robert Peel public house,<sup id="cite_ref-106" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-106">&#91;106&#93;</a></sup> <a href="/wiki/Stoke-on-Trent" title="Stoke-on-Trent">Stoke-on-Trent</a>.</li>
<li>Sir Robert Peel public house, <a href="/wiki/Kingston_upon_Thames" title="Kingston upon Thames">Kingston upon Thames</a>, Surrey.</li>
<li>Sir Robert Peel public house, <a href="/wiki/Bloxwich" title="Bloxwich">Bloxwich</a>, <a href="/wiki/Walsall" title="Walsall">Walsall</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-107" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-107">&#91;107&#93;</a></sup></li></ul>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Elsewhere">Elsewhere</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Robert_Peel&amp;action=edit&amp;section=27" title="Edit section: Elsewhere">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<ul><li>The Sir Robert Peel Hotel (colloquially known as "The Peel"), a gay bar and nightclub located at the corner of Peel and Wellington Streets in the Melbourne suburb of <a href="/wiki/Collingwood,_Victoria" title="Collingwood, Victoria">Collingwood</a>, in Australia.</li>
<li>The Sir Robert Peel Hotel on the corner of Queensberry Street and Peel Street in the Melbourne suburb of <a href="/wiki/North_Melbourne,_Victoria" class="mw-redirect" title="North Melbourne, Victoria">North Melbourne, Victoria</a>, in Australia.</li>
<li>The Sir Robert Peel Motor Lodge Hotel, <a href="/wiki/Alexandria_Bay,_New_York" title="Alexandria Bay, New York">Alexandria Bay, New York</a>.</li></ul>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Other_memorials">Other memorials</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Robert_Peel&amp;action=edit&amp;section=28" title="Edit section: Other memorials">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Peel_Park,_Bradford" title="Peel Park, Bradford">Peel Park, Bradford</a> is named after Sir Robert Peel. It is one of the largest parks in the city, and indeed <a href="/wiki/Yorkshire" title="Yorkshire">Yorkshire</a>.</li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Peel_Monument" title="Peel Monument">Peel Tower Monument</a>, built on top of Holcombe Hill in <a href="/wiki/Ramsbottom" title="Ramsbottom">Ramsbottom</a>, <a href="/wiki/Metropolitan_Borough_of_Bury" title="Metropolitan Borough of Bury">Bury</a>.</li>
<li>The Sir Robert Peel Hospital in <a href="/wiki/Tamworth,_Staffordshire" title="Tamworth, Staffordshire">Tamworth</a>.</li>
<li>A small monument in the centre of the town of <a href="/wiki/Dronfield" title="Dronfield">Dronfield</a> in Derbyshire. Nearby is the Peel Centre, a community centre in a former Methodist church.<sup id="cite_ref-108" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-108">&#91;108&#93;</a></sup></li>
<li>Peel Streets in the CBD of <a href="/wiki/Melbourne" title="Melbourne">Melbourne</a>, and in <a href="/wiki/Collingwood,_Victoria" title="Collingwood, Victoria">Collingwood</a>, both in <a href="/wiki/Victoria_(Australia)" class="mw-redirect" title="Victoria (Australia)">Victoria</a>, Australia.</li>
<li>Peel Street in the CBD, <a href="/wiki/Adelaide,_South_Australia" class="mw-redirect" title="Adelaide, South Australia">Adelaide, South Australia</a>.</li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Peel_Street,_Montreal" class="mw-redirect" title="Peel Street, Montreal">Peel Street, Montreal</a> and its <a href="/wiki/Peel_(Montreal_Metro)" class="mw-redirect" title="Peel (Montreal Metro)">Peel Metro station</a>. The street also features a high-rise residential building named Sir-Robert-Peel.</li>
<li>The <a href="/wiki/Peel_River_(New_South_Wales)" title="Peel River (New South Wales)">Peel River</a> in <a href="/wiki/Tamworth,_New_South_Wales" title="Tamworth, New South Wales">Tamworth, New South Wales</a>, Australia.</li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Peel_High_School" title="Peel High School">Peel High School</a> in <a href="/wiki/Tamworth,_New_South_Wales" title="Tamworth, New South Wales">Tamworth, New South Wales</a>, Australia.</li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Peel,_New_South_Wales" title="Peel, New South Wales">Peel</a> in <a href="/wiki/Tamworth,_New_South_Wales" title="Tamworth, New South Wales">New South Wales</a>, Australia.</li>
<li>Robert Peel Primary School in <a href="/wiki/Sandy,_Bedfordshire" title="Sandy, Bedfordshire">Sandy, Bedfordshire</a>.</li>
<li>A British steamer named <i>SS Sir Robert Peel</i>, based in Canada, was burned by American forces on 29 May 1838, at the height of American-Canadian tensions over the <a href="/wiki/Caroline_Affair" class="mw-redirect" title="Caroline Affair">Caroline Affair</a>.</li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Tamworth,_Staffordshire" title="Tamworth, Staffordshire">Tamworth</a>-raised musician <a href="/wiki/Julian_Cope" title="Julian Cope">Julian Cope</a> sings "the king and queen have offered me the estate of Robert Peel" on the song "Laughing Boy", from his 1984 LP <i><a href="/wiki/Fried_(album)" title="Fried (album)">Fried</a></i>.</li>
<li>The right wing of the <a href="/wiki/Trafford_Centre" title="Trafford Centre">Trafford Centre</a> is called Peel Avenue, named after Robert Peel.</li>
<li>The official mascot of <a href="/wiki/Bury_Football_Club" class="mw-redirect" title="Bury Football Club">Bury Football Club</a> is Robbie the Bobby, in honour of Sir Robert Peel.</li>
<li>One of the buildings which make up the <a href="/wiki/Home_Office" title="Home Office">Home Office</a> headquarters, <a href="/wiki/2_Marsham_Street" title="2 Marsham Street">2 Marsham St</a>, is named Peel.</li>
<li>The Peel building, situated on Peel Campus of the <a href="/wiki/University_of_Salford" title="University of Salford">University of Salford</a>.</li>
<li>The Sir Robert Peel monument on the corner of George and High Streets, Montrose, Scotland</li>
<li>Peel Crescent in Mansfield, Nottinghamshire, UK.</li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Peel_Street,_Hong_Kong" title="Peel Street, Hong Kong">Peel Street, Hong Kong</a>, a small street in Hong Kong.</li>
<li>Peel Street in Simcoe, Ontario, Canada is named in his honour.</li>
<li>The <a href="/wiki/Regional_Municipality_of_Peel" title="Regional Municipality of Peel">Regional Municipality of Peel</a> (originally Peel County) in <a href="/wiki/Ontario" title="Ontario">Ontario</a>, Canada.
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/10_Peel_Centre_Drive" title="10 Peel Centre Drive">10 Peel Centre Drive</a> and Peel Centre.</li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Peel_Regional_Police" title="Peel Regional Police">Peel Regional Police</a>.</li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Peel_Regional_Paramedic_Services" title="Peel Regional Paramedic Services">Peel Regional Paramedic Services</a>.</li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Dufferin-Peel_Catholic_District_School_Board" title="Dufferin-Peel Catholic District School Board">Dufferin-Peel Catholic District School Board</a>.</li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Peel_District_School_Board" title="Peel District School Board">Peel District School Board</a>.</li>
<li>former <a href="/wiki/Peel_Memorial_Hospital" title="Peel Memorial Hospital">Peel Memorial Hospital</a> (closed 2007) in <a href="/wiki/Brampton,_Ontario" class="mw-redirect" title="Brampton, Ontario">Brampton, Ontario</a>.</li></ul></li>
<li>New Zealand pioneer <a href="/wiki/Francis_Jollie" title="Francis Jollie">Francis Jollie</a> settled in <a href="/wiki/Canterbury_Region" title="Canterbury Region">Canterbury</a> in 1853 and named <a href="/wiki/Peel_Forest_Park" class="mw-redirect" title="Peel Forest Park">Peel Forest</a> after the former prime minister, as he had died in the year that Canterbury was founded. The <a href="/wiki/Mount_Peel" title="Mount Peel">adjacent mountain</a> and the <a href="/wiki/Peel_Forest" title="Peel Forest">settlement that formed</a> also took Peel's name.<sup id="cite_ref-FOOTNOTEReed2010310_109-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-FOOTNOTEReed2010310-109">&#91;109&#93;</a></sup></li>
<li>The names "bobbies" and "peelers" for <a href="/wiki/British_police" class="mw-redirect" title="British police">British police</a> officers.<sup id="cite_ref-110" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-110">&#91;110&#93;</a></sup></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Peel%27s_Acts" title="Peel&#39;s Acts">Peel's Acts</a> are named after Peel.</li></ul>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="In_literature">In literature</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Robert_Peel&amp;action=edit&amp;section=29" title="Edit section: In literature">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p><a href="/wiki/Letitia_Elizabeth_Landon" title="Letitia Elizabeth Landon">Letitia Elizabeth Landon</a> gave her tribute to Sir Robert in her poetical illustration <i>Sir Robert Peel</i> to <a href="/wiki/Thomas_Lawrence" title="Thomas Lawrence">Thomas Lawrence</a>'s portrait in Fisher's Drawing Room Scrap Book, 1837.<sup id="cite_ref-111" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-111">&#91;111&#93;</a></sup>
</p>

<div class="side-box-flex">

<p>Robert Peel is a secondary character in the novel <i>Dodger</i> by <a href="/wiki/Terry_Pratchett" title="Terry Pratchett">Terry Pratchett</a>.
</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Arms">Arms</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Robert_Peel&amp;action=edit&amp;section=30" title="Edit section: Arms">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<table class="wikitable mw-collapsible" style="max-width: 100%">
<caption class="nowrap">Coat of arms of Robert Peel
</caption>
<tbody><tr>
<td style="width: 220px;"><span class="mw-default-size" typeof="mw:File/Frameless"><a href="/wiki/File:Robert_Peel_Coat_of_Arms.svg" class="mw-file-description"><img src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/3/3b/Robert_Peel_Coat_of_Arms.svg/220px-Robert_Peel_Coat_of_Arms.svg.png" decoding="async" width="220" height="277" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/3/3b/Robert_Peel_Coat_of_Arms.svg/330px-Robert_Peel_Coat_of_Arms.svg.png 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/3/3b/Robert_Peel_Coat_of_Arms.svg/440px-Robert_Peel_Coat_of_Arms.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="696" data-file-height="877" /></a></span>
</td>
<td style="min-width:175px;">
<dl><dt>Crest</dt>
<dd>A demi lion rampant Argent gorged with a collar Azure charged with three bezants, holding between the paws a shuttle Or.</dd>
<dt>Escutcheon</dt>
<dd>Argent three sheaves of as many arrows Proper banded Gules; on a chief Azure a bee volant, Or.</dd>
<dt>Motto</dt>
<dd><b>INDUSTRIA</b><sup id="cite_ref-112" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-112">&#91;112&#93;</a></sup></dd></dl>
</td></tr></tbody></table>
